       case \"moon\": {\n          const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2, cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n          ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.abs(w / 2), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n          ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(cx + Math.abs(w) * 0.22, cy - Math.abs(h) * 0.08, Math.abs(w / 2) * 0.92, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n          ctx.stroke();\n          break;\n        }\n        case \"callout\":\n          ctx.rect(x1, y1, w, h * 0.7);\n          ctx.moveTo(x1 + Math.abs(w) * 0.2, y1 + h * 0.7);\n          ctx.lineTo(x1 + Math.abs(w) * 0.2, y2);\n          ctx.lineTo(x1 + Math.abs(w) * 0.45, y1 + h * 0.7);\n          ctx.stroke();\n          break;\n        case \"human\": {\n          const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n          const top = Math.min(y1, y2);\n          ctx.arc(cx, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.12, Math.abs(h) * 0.12, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.moveTo(cx, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.24); ctx.lineTo(cx, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.62);\n          ctx.moveTo(cx - Math.abs(w) * 0.25, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.38); ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.abs(w) * 0.25, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.38);\n          ctx.moveTo(cx, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.62); ctx.lineTo(cx - Math.abs(w) * 0.25, y2);\n          ctx.moveTo(cx, top + Math.abs(h) * 0.62); ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.abs(w) * 0.25, y2);\n          ctx.stroke();\n          break;\n        }\n        default:\n \"destination-out\";\n        ctx.lineWidth = brushSize * 3;\n      } else {\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = \"source-over\";\n        ctx.strokeStyle = tool === \"pencil\" ? color : color;\n        ctx.lineWidth = tool === \"pencil\" ? 1 : brushSize;\n      }\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(from.x, from.y);\n      ctx.lineTo(to.x, to.y);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.restore();\n    },\n    [active, brushSize, color, tool]\n  );\n\n  const floodFill = useCallback(\n    (pt: Point) => {\n      const rt = active();\n      if (!rt) return;\n      const ctx = rt.canvas.getContext(\"2d\")!;\n      const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, rt.canvas.width, rt.canvas.height);\n      const { width: w, height: h, data: p } = img;\n      const sx = Math.floor(pt.x), sy = Math.floor(pt.y);\n      if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= w || sy >= h) return;\n      const idx = (x: number, y: number) => (y * w + x) * 4;\n      const start = idx(sx, sy);\n      const target = [p[start], p[start + 1], p[start + 2], p[start + 3]];\n      const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(color);\n      const fill = [r, g, b, 255];\n      if (target.every((v, i) => Math.abs(v - fill[i]) < 4)) return;\n      const match = (i: number) =>\n        Math.abs(p[i] - target[0]) < 24 &&\n        Math.abs(p[i + 1] - target[1]) < 24 &&\n        Math.abs(p[i + 2] - target[2]) < 24 &&\n        Math.abs(p[i + 3] - target[3]) < 24;\n      const stack: number[] = [sx, sy];\n      const seen = new Uint8Array(w * h);\n      while (stack.length) {\n        const y = stack.pop()!;\n        const x = stack.pop()!;\n        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= w || y >= h) continue;\n        if (seen[y * w + x]) continue;\n        const i = idx(x, y);\n        if (!match(i)) continue;\n        seen[y * w + x] = 1;\n        p[i] = fill[0]; p[i + 1] = fill[1]; p[i + 2] = fill[2]; p[i + 3] = fill[3];\n        stack.push(x + 1, y, x - 1, y, x, y + 1, x, y - 1);\n      }\n      ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);\n    },\n    [active, color]\n  );\n\n  const onPointerDown = useCallback(\n    (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n      const pt = toImageCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);\n      if (!pt)    if (tool === \"fill\") {\n        pushHistory();\n        floodFill(pt);\n        setStatus(\"Fill\");\n        syncMeta();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (tool === \"crop\" || tool ===       ctx.fillText(val, pt.x, pt.y);\n          syncMeta();\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n      if ([\"blur\", \"sharpen\", \"desaturate\", \"bulge_pinch\", \"clone\"].includes(tool)) {\n        pushHistory();\n        applyToActive(\n          (d) => {\n            const { width: w, data: p } = d;\n            const radius = 18;\n            const copy = new Uint8ClampedArray(p);\n      const v = 0.299 * copy[i] + 0.587 * copy[i + 1] + 0.114 * copy[i + 2];\n                  p[i] = p[i + 1] = p[i + 2] = v;\n                } else if (tool === \"sharpen\") {\n               p[i + c] = cur + (cur - avg) * 0.8;\n                  }\n                } else if (tool === \"blur\") {\n                  for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {\n                    p[i + c] =       syncMeta();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (tool === \"gradient\") {\n        drag.current = { start: pt, last: pt, active: true, preview: true };\n        return;\n      }\n      if (tool );\n\n  const onPointerMove = useCallback(\n    (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n      const pt = toImageCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);\n      if (!pt) return;\n      setMouse(pt);\n (action: string) => void;\n}\n\nfunction isLeaf(e: MenuEntry): e is MenuLeaf {\n  return e !== \"separator\";\n}\n\nexport function MainMenu({ dispatch }: Props) {\n  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [openSub, setOpenSub] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const onDocDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {\n        setOpenId(null);\n        setOpenSub(null);\n      }\n    };\n    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.key === \"Escape\") {\n        setOpenId(null);\n        setOpenSub(null);\n      }\n    };\n    document.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocDown);\n    document.addEventListener(\"keydown\", onKey);\n    return () => {\n      document.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocDown);\n      document.removeEventListener(\"keydown\", onKey);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const runItem = (leaf: MenuLeaf) => {\n    if (leaf.sub) return;\n    setOpenId(null);\n    setOpenSub(null);\n    if (leaf.action) dispatch(leaf.action);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <nav className=\"mp-menubar\" aria-label=\"Main Menu\" ref={ref}>\n      <ul role=\"menubar\">\n        {MENU.map((menu) => {\n          const open = openId === menu.id;\n          return (\n aria-expanded={open}\n                data-open={open}\n                className={cn(\"mp-menu-title\", open && \"is-open\")}\n                onClick={() => {\n                  setOpenId(open ? null : menu.id);\n                  setOpenSub(null);\n                }}\n                onMouseEnter={() => {\n                  if (openId && openId !== menu.id) {\n                    setOpenId(menu.id);\n                    setOpenSub(null);\n                  }\n                }}\n              >\n                {menu.label}\n              </button>\n              {open && (\n  <li key={id} role=\"none\" style={{ position: \"relative\" }}>\n                        <button\n                          type=\"button\"\n                          role=\"menuitem\"\n                          aria-haspopup={hasSub || undefined}\n                          aria-expanded={hasSub ? openSub === id : undefined}\n                          className=\"mp-menu-item\"\n (\n                            <span className=\"mp-sub-arrow\">&gt;</span>\n                          ) : entry.shortcut ? [{"type": "tool_use", "id": "call_00_ET_U1lUUunkrJKx2fKowwFO1788", "name": "Write", "input": {"file_path": "/workspace/recreation/src/components/ShapePreview.tsx", "content": "import { useEffect, useRef } from \"react\";\nimport type { Point } from \"@/editor/types\";\n\n/** Draw a filled shape outline into a canvas context, in the given box. */\nexport function paintShape(\n  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n  shape: string,\n  box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },\n  opts: { fill?: string; stroke?: string; lineWidth?: number; filled?: boolean } = {}\n) {\n  const { fill = \"#d9dde0\", stroke = \"#4a5254\", lineWidth = 2, filled = true } = opts;\n  const x1 = box.x;\n  const y1 = box.y;\n  const x2 = box.x + box.w;\n  const y2 = box.y + box.h;\n  ctx.save();\n  ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;\n  ctx.lineJoin = \"round\";\n  ctx.lineCap = \"round\";\n  const w = box.w;\n  const h = box.h;\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  switch (shape) {\n    case \"line\":\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);\n      break;\n    case \"arrow\": {\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);\n      const ang = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);\n      const hl = Math.min(w, h) * 0.28;\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2 - hl * Math.cos(ang - 0.45), y2 - hl * Math.sin(ang - 0.45));\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2 - hl * Math.cos(ang + 0.45), y2 - hl * Math.sin(ang + 0.45));\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"callout\": {\n      const bh = h * 0.72;\n      ctx.rect(x1, y1, w, bh);\n      ctx.moveTo(x1 + w * 0.12, y1 + bh);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1 + w * 0.12, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1 + w * 0.42, y1 + bh);\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"cog\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      const R = Math.min(w, h) / 2;\n      const teeth = 9;\n      for (let i = 0; i <= teeth * 2; i++) {\n        const a = (i * Math.PI) / teeth;\n        const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? R : R * 0.78;\n        const px = cx + rad * Math.cos(a);\n        const py = cy + rad * Math.sin(a);\n        i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(px, py) : ctx.lineTo(px, py);\n      }\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx + R * 0.32, cy);\n      ctx.arc(cx, cy, R * 0.32, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"cylinder\": {\n      const rx = w / 2;\n      const ry = Math.min(h / 4, 16);\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1 + ry);\n      ctx.ellipse((x1 + x2) / 2, y1 + ry, rx, ry, 0, Math.PI, 0, true);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2 - ry);\n      ctx.ellipse((x1 + x2) / 2, y2 - ry, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI, true);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, y1 + ry);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1 + ry);\n      ctx.ellipse((x1 + x2) / 2, y1 + ry, rx, ry, 0, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2, true);\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"ellipse\":\n      ctx.ellipse((x1 + x2) / 2, (y1 + y2) / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      break;\n    case \"heart\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      const sx = w / 2;\n      const sy = h / 2;\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy + sy * 0.8);\n      ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - sx * 1.4, cy - sy * 0.4, cx - sx * 0.5, cy - sy * 1.2, cx, cy - sy * 0.35);\n      ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + sx * 0.5, cy - sy * 1.2, cx + sx * 1.4, cy - sy * 0.4, cx, cy + sy * 0.8);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"hexagon\":\n    case \"pentagon\":\n    case \"polygon\": {\n      const sides = shape === \"hexagon\" ? 6 : shape === \"pentagon\" ? 5 : 6;\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      const rx = w / 2;\n      const ry = h / 2;\n      for (let i = 0; i <= sides; i++) {\n        const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * 2 * Math.PI) / sides;\n        const px = cx + rx * Math.cos(a);\n        const py = cy + ry * Math.sin(a);\n        i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(px, py) : ctx.lineTo(px, py);\n      }\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"human\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      ctx.moveTo(cx + h * 0.11, y1 + h * 0.12);\n      ctx.arc(cx, y1 + h * 0.12, h * 0.11, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, y1 + h * 0.23);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx, y1 + h * 0.6);\n      ctx.moveTo(cx - w * 0.24, y1 + h * 0.36);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + w * 0.24, y1 + h * 0.36);\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, y1 + h * 0.6);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx - w * 0.24, y2);\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, y1 + h * 0.6);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + w * 0.24, y2);\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"moon\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      const R = Math.min(w, h) / 2;\n      ctx.arc(cx, cy, R, -Math.PI * 0.5, Math.PI * 0.5, false);\n      ctx.arc(cx + R * 0.42, cy, R * 0.86, Math.PI * 0.5, -Math.PI * 0.5, true);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"parallelogram\":\n      ctx.moveTo(x1 + w * 0.24, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2 - w * 0.24, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, y2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    case \"plus\": {\n      const t = Math.min(w, h) / 3;\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      ctx.moveTo(cx - t / 2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + t / 2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + t / 2, cy - t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, cy - t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, cy + t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + t / 2, cy + t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx + t / 2, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx - t / 2, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx - t / 2, cy + t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, cy + t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, cy - t / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(cx - t / 2, cy - t / 2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"rectangle\":\n      ctx.rect(x1, y1, w, h);\n      break;\n    case \"right_triangle\":\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    case \"romb\":\n      ctx.moveTo((x1 + x2) / 2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, (y1 + y2) / 2);\n      ctx.lineTo((x1 + x2) / 2, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, (y1 + y2) / 2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    case \"star\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      const cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;\n      const R = Math.min(w, h) / 2;\n      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n        const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI) / 5;\n        const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? R : R * 0.42;\n        const px = cx + rad * Math.cos(a);\n        const py = cy + rad * Math.sin(a);\n        i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(px, py) : ctx.lineTo(px, py);\n      }\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"tear\": {\n      const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, y1);\n      ctx.bezierCurveTo(x2, y1 + h * 0.4, x2, y1 + h * 0.7, x2 - w * 0.15, y2 - h * 0.18);\n      ctx.bezierCurveTo(x1 + w * 0.15, y2 + h * 0.05, x1 + w * 0.15, y1 + h * 0.35, cx, y1);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"trapezoid\":\n      ctx.moveTo(x1 + w * 0.2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2 - w * 0.2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, y2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    case \"triangle\":\n      ctx.moveTo((x1 + x2) / 2, y1);\n      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x1, y2);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      break;\n    case \"bezier_curve\": {\n      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);\n      ctx.bezierCurveTo(x2, y1, x1, y2, x2, y2);\n      break;\n    }\n    default:\n      ctx.rect(x1, y1, w, h);\n  }\n  if (filled && stroke !== \"none\") {\n    ctx.fillStyle = fill;\n    ctx.fill();\n  }\n  if (stroke !== \"none\") {\n    ctx.strokeStyle = stroke;\n    ctx.stroke();\n  }\n  ctx.restore();\n}\n\nexport function ShapePreview({ shape, size = 150, height = 120 }: { shape: string; size?: number; height?: number }) {\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const c = ref.current;\n    if (!c) return;\n    const ctx = c.getContext(\"2d\", { willReadFrequently: true })!;\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);\n    const pad = 14;\n    paintShape(\n      ctx,\n      shape,\n      { x: pad, y: pad, w: c.width - pad * 2, h: c.height - pad * 2 },\n      { fill: \"#d8dcde\", stroke: \"#3f4648\", lineWidth: 2.5 }\n    );\n  }, [shape]);\n\n  return <canvas ref={ref} width={size} height={height} className=\"effectsPreview\" />;\n}\n\nexport type { Point };\n"}}]
